     OTHER FINANCIAL INFORMATION                                                         其他財務資料
     Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                         非國際財務報告準則財務計量
     To supplement our consolidated results, which are presented in accordance           為補充根據國際財務報告準則呈列的綜合業
     with IFRS, we also use certain non-IFRS measures, namely adjusted EBITDA            績，我們亦使用若干非國際財務報告準則計量
     and adjusted net profit for the period, as additional financial metrics. These      （即經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額）作為
     non-IFRS financial measures are not required by or presented in accordance          額外財務指標。該等非國際財務報告準則財務
     with IFRS.                                                                          計量並非國際財務報告準則所規定，亦並非按
                                                                                         其呈列。


     Adjusted EBITDA is defined as operating profit adjusted for (i) share-based         經調整EBITDA定義為經以下項目調整的經營溢
     compensation, gross; (ii) amortization of intangible assets; (iii) depreciation     利：(i)以股份為基礎的酬金總額；(ii)無形資產攤
     of property, plant and equipment, and right-of-use assets; (iv) foreign             銷；(iii)物業、廠房及設備和使用權資產折舊；
     exchange (gain)/loss, net; (v) net (gains)/losses on investees; and (vi) others,    (iv)匯兌（收益）╱ 虧損淨額；(v)被投資方的（收
     including acquisition-related cost and net gains on foreign currency financial      益）╱ 虧損淨額；及(vi)其他，包括收購相關成
     instruments. Adjusted net profit for the period is defined as profit for the        本及外幣金融工具的淨收益。期內經調整溢利
     period adjusted for (i) share-based compensation, net; (ii) the amortization of     淨額定義為經以下項目調整的期內溢利：(i)以股
     intangible assets from acquisition; (iii) foreign exchange (gain)/loss, net; (iv)   份為基礎的酬金淨額；(ii)收購產生的無形資產

     The above items are excluded from our adjusted EBITDA and adjusted net              上述項目從經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利
     profit for the period measures because these items are either non-cash in           淨額的計量中剔除乃由於該等項目屬於非現金
     nature, or are not driven by core operations which render comparisons with          性質，或並非受核心業務所驅動，導致其與過
     prior periods and competitors less meaningful. We believe the adjusted              往期間及競爭對手的比較意義不大。我們認為
     EBITDA and adjusted net profit for the period are useful measures for the           經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額對分析師
     analysts and investors as basis for evaluation of our future on-going operating     和投資者而言屬有用的計量指標，可作為評估
     performance as these measures allow more meaningful comparison of our               我們未來持續經營表現的依據，原因是該等計
     performance and projected cash earnings with our historical results from            量指標可讓我們的表現及預測現金收益與我們
     prior periods and to the results of our competitors. Moreover, management           過往期間的歷史業績及競爭對手的業績進行更
     uses these measures internally to evaluate the performance of our business          具意義的比較。此外，管理層內部使用該等計
     as a whole. However, our presentation of such non-IFRS measures may not             量指標來評估我們的整體業務表現。然而，該
     be comparable to similarly titled measures presented by other companies.            等非國際財務報告準則計量的呈列未必可與其
     The use of these non-IFRS measures has limitations as an analytical tool, and       他公司所列類似計量指標相比。該等非國際財

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                            外匯風險
Foreign exchange risk arises when future commercial transactions or              當未來的商業交易或已確認的資產及負債不是
recognized assets and liabilities are denominated in a currency that is not      以我們的功能貨幣計價時，即產生外匯風險。
our functional currency. We manage our foreign exchange risk by performing       我們通過定期審查我們的外匯淨風險敞口管理
regular reviews of our net foreign exchange exposures.                           外匯風險。


Our subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities in the PRC operate         我們的中國附屬公司及併表聯屬實體主要在中
mainly in the PRC with most of the transactions settled in RMB. There are        國境內經營，而大部分交易以人民幣結算。
certain financial assets or liabilities of our Group are denominated in the      本集團有若干財務資產或負債以本集團於中國
currencies other than the respective functional currencies of our Group’s        營運的附屬公司及併表聯屬實體各自功能貨幣
subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities operating in the PRC, and      以外的貨幣計值，而截至二零二五年六月三十
as of June 30, 2025, the Group entered into certain derivative contracts with    日，本集團與金融機構訂立若干用作經濟對沖
financial institutions which are used for economic hedging purposes. These       用途的衍生合約。該等衍生合約列作衍生金融
derivative contracts are accounted for as derivative financial instruments and   工具並以公允價值計量。
measured at fair value.

     The Company has approved and adopted the 2018 Share Incentive Plan by              本公司已於二零一八年三月九日通過董事會決
     the Board’s resolution on March 9, 2018. No additional options have been or        議案批准及採納二零一八年股份激勵計劃。上
     will be granted under the 2018 Share Incentive Plan after the Listing.             市後未曾或概不會根據二零一八年股份激勵計

     Purpose                                                                            目的
     The purpose of the 2018 Share Incentive Plan is to promote the success             二零一八年股份激勵計劃旨在通過將本公司及
     and enhance the value of our Company, by linking the personal interests of         本公司任何現時或未來附屬公司或VIE（各定義見
     the directors, officers and employees of our Company and any present or            下文）董事、高級職員及僱員的個人利益掛鈎，
     future subsidiaries or VIEs (each term as defined below) of our Company by         藉著激勵該等人士作出傑出表現，為股東帶來
     providing such individuals with an incentive for outstanding performance,          豐厚回報，以促進本公司的成功及提升其價
     to generate superior returns to the Shareholders. The 2018 Share Incentive         值。二零一八年股份激勵計劃亦旨在使本公司
     Plan is further intended to provide flexibility to our Company in its ability to   能靈活激勵、吸引及留用激勵對象提供服務，
     motivate, attract, and retain the services of recipients upon whose judgment,      而本公司能否成功經營業務，將主要取決於該
     interest, and special effort the successful conduct of our Company’s               等人士的判斷、利益及特別努力。
     operation is largely dependent.

     2019 RSU PLAN                                                                     二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃
     The Company has approved and adopted the 2019 RSU Plan by the Board’s             本公司已於二零一九年七月二日（「二零一九年
     resolution on July 2, 2019 (the “Effective Date of the 2019 RSU Plan”).           受限制股份單位計劃生效日期」）以董事會決議
                                                                                       案批准及採納二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃。


     As at the beginning and the end of the Reporting Period, no RSUs were             於報告期初及期末，概無根據二零一九年受限
     granted but yet to be vested under the 2019 RSU Plan. During the Reporting        制股份單位計劃授出但尚未歸屬的受限制股份
     Period, no RSUs were granted, vested, cancelled or lapsed under the 2019          單位。於報告期內，概無根據二零一九年受限
     RSU Plan.                                                                         制股份單位計劃授出、歸屬、註銷或失效的受
                                                                                       限制股份單位。


     Purpose                                                                           目的
     The purpose of the 2019 RSU Plan is to provide better rewards to the talents      二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃旨在向本集團
     who have contributed to the development and success of the Group, and for         過往及未來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更
     the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents to continue         好的回報，通過提供購買股份權益的機會，激
     to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through providing them      勵該等人才繼續竭盡所能及招攬新人才。
     with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares.


     Eligible participants                                                             合資格參與者
     The eligible participants of the 2019 RSU Plan include (i) any Director, (ii)     二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃的合資格參與

2024 SHARE SCHEME                                                                 二零二四年股份計劃
The Company has adopted the 2024 Share Scheme by an ordinary resolution           本公司已於二零二四年六月二十六日以股東正
duly passed by the Shareholders on June 26, 2024. As at the Latest Practicable    式通過的普通決議案採納二零二四年股份計
Date, save for the grant made on June 27, 2024 to Mr. Ma Heping, no share         劃。於最後實際可行日期，除於二零二四年六
options or awards were granted, vested, exercised, lapsed or cancelled under      月二十七日授予馬和平先生外，概無根據二零
the 2024 Share Scheme.                                                            二四年股份計劃授出、歸屬、行使、失效或註
                                                                                  銷購股權或獎勵。


Purpose                                                                           目的
The purpose of the 2024 Share Scheme is (a) to provide the Company                二零二四年股份計劃旨在(a)向本公司提供薪
with flexible means of remunerating, incentivizing, retaining, rewarding,         酬、激勵、挽留、獎勵、補償及 ╱ 或向合資格
compensating and/or providing benefits to Eligible Participants (as defined       參與者（定義見下文）提供利益的靈活方式；(b)
below); (b) to align the interests of Eligible Participants with those of the     通過向合資格參與者提供購買本公司股權的機
Company and Shareholders by providing such Eligible Participants with the         會，使該等合資格參與者的利益與本公司及股
opportunity to acquire shareholding interests in the Company; and (c) to          東的利益保持一致；及(c)鼓勵合資格參與者為
encourage Eligible Participants to contribute to the long-term growth and         本公司的長期增長及盈利能力作出貢獻，並為
profitability of the Company and to enhance the value of the Company and          本公司及股東的整體利益提升本公司及其股份
its Shares for the benefit of the Company and Shareholders as a whole.            的價值。


Eligible Participants                                                             合資格參與者

Participants”) include (i) employee participants, being any person who is an      參與者」）包括(i)僱員參與者，即本集團任何成
employee (whether full-time or part-time), director or officer of any member      員公司的任何僱員（不論全職或兼職）、董事或
of the Group; and (ii) related entity participants, being any person who is an    高級職員；及(ii)關聯實體參與者，即本公司聯
employee (whether full-time or part-time), director or officer of an associated   營公司的任何僱員（不論全職或兼職）、董事或
company of the Company.                                                           高級職員。

     We conducted our review in accordance with International Standard                 我們已根據國際審閱準則第2410號「由實體的獨

     Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity”. A review of interim          閱中期財務資料包括主要向負責財務和會計事
     financial information consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons          務的人員作出查詢，及應用分析性和其他審閱
     responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical         程序。審閱的範圍遠較根據《國際審計準則》進
     and other review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an      行審計的範圍為小，故不能令我們可保證我們
     audit conducted in accordance with International Standards on Auditing            將知悉在審計中可能被發現的所有重大事項。
     and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would             因此，我們不會發表審計意見。
     become aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit.

      5. FAIR VALUE ESTIMATION (CONTINUED)                                                      （續）
                                                                                        5. 公允價值估計
          (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                           (a) 第1層金融工具
              The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets is          於活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公允價
              based on quoted market prices at the balance sheet date. A market            值乃基於結算日市場報價。倘交易
              is regarded as active if quoted prices are readily and regularly             所、交易商、經紀、行業組織、定價
              available from an exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing          服務機構或監管機構隨時定期提供報
              service, or regulatory agency, and those prices represent actual and         價，且該等價格代表實際及定期進行
              regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length basis.            的公平市場交易，則市場被視為活躍。


          (b) Financial instruments in Level 2                                           (b) 第2層金融工具
              The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an            並無於活躍市場買賣的金融工具公允
              active market is determined by using valuation techniques. These             價值以估值技術釐定。該等估值技術
              valuation techniques maximize the use of observable market                   盡量利用既有可觀察市場數據，盡量
              data where it is available and rely as little as possible on entity          少依賴實體的特定估計。倘計算某工
              specific estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair value an      具的公允價值所需的所有重大輸入值
              instrument are observable, the instrument is included in Level 2.            為可觀察數據，則該工具將列入第2
                                                                                           層。


          (c) Financial instruments in Level 3                                           (c) 第3層金融工具
              If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on observable          倘一項或多項重大輸入值並非基於可
              market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                          觀察市場數據，則該工具計入第3層。


              Specific valuation techniques used to value financial instruments            用於評估金融工具的專門估值技術包
              include:                                                                     括：


              •    Quoted market prices or dealer quotes for similar instruments.          •   市場報價或交易商對類似工具的報
                                                                                               價。


              •    Other techniques, such as discounted cash flow or expected              •   貼現現金流量或預期可收回現金流
                   recoverable cash flows analysis are used to determine fair value            量分析等其他技術用於釐定金融工
                   for financial instruments.                                                  具公允價值。


              Level 3 instruments of the Group’s financial assets include long-            本集團金融資產的第3層工具包括按公
              term or short-term investments measured at fair value through                允價值計量且其變動計入損益的長期
              profit or loss and certain derivative financial instruments.                 或短期投資及若干衍生金融工具。

  The Group has identified two reportable segments, namely (i) Core OTA          本集團已識別兩個可呈報分部，即(i)核心在
  segment, which comprising the core online travel agency and related            線旅遊平台分部，包括核心在線旅遊平台
  businesses; and (ii) Tourism segment, which comprising the offline travel      及相關業務；及(ii)度假分部，包括線下旅
  agency and tourism related businesses. The chief operating decision-           行社及度假相關業務。主要營運決策者（即
  makers (i.e. the Chief Executive Officer, Vice Presidents and the Directors    本公司首席執行官、副總裁及董事）

  of the Company) (the “CODMs”) review and evaluate the business                 營運決策者」）分開審閱及評估該兩項業務
  performance of these two businesses separately for allocating resources        的業務表現，以分配資源及作出戰略決策。
  and making strategic decisions.


  The CODMs assess the performance of the operating segments mainly              主要營運決策者主要根據各營運分部的收

  is in line with CODMs’ performance review. There were no material inter-       這與主要營運決策者的表現審閱一致。截
  segment sales during the six months ended June 30, 2025 and 2024.              至二零二五年及二零二四年六月三十日止

  compensation expenses, amortisation of those intangible assets as              配 項 目（主 要 關 於 以 股 份 為 基 礎 的 酬 金 開
  identified from business combinations, certain corporate administrative        支、業務合併中識別的無形資產攤銷、若

17. DERIVATIVE FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                               17. 衍生金融工具
  The Group has entered into certain derivative contracts with financial                 本集團與金融機構訂立若干用作經濟對沖
  institutions which are used for economic hedging purposes (instead of                  用 途（ 而 非 投 機 性 投 資 ）的 衍 生 合 約 。 然
  being speculative investments). However, these derivative contracts do                 而，該等衍生合約不符合對沖會計準則，
  not meet the hedging accounting criteria and hence they are classified                 因而就會計目的而言分類為「持作買賣」，
  as ‘held for trading’ for accounting purposes and are accounted for as                 並入賬列作按公允價值計量且其變動計入
  financial assets/liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.                     損益的金融資產 ╱ 負債。


  The current assets of derivative financial instruments still outstanding               截至二零二五年六月三十日及二零二四年
  as of June 30, 2025 and December 31, 2024 is RMB5,201,000 and                          十二月三十一日仍未到期的衍生金融工具
  RMB6,194,000 respectively. The current liabilities of derivative financial             的流動資產分別為人民幣5,201,000元及人民
  instruments still outstanding as of June 30, 2025 is RMB2,867,000 (as of               幣6,194,000元。截至二零二五年六月三十日
  December 31, 2024: Nil).                                                               仍未償還的衍生金融工具的流動負債為人

                                                                                       21. 貿易應收款項
          The Group normally allows a credit period of 30 days to its customers.        本集團通常給予其客戶 30 天的信貸期。根
          An ageing analysis of trade receivables based on invoice date is as           據發票日期對貿易應收款項進行的賬齡分
          follows:                                                                      析如下：

          Over 6 months                                    六個月以上                                  102,575          117,550


                                                                                                 2,205,473       1,820,170


          Trade receivables are classified as financial assets measured at amortized    貿易應收款項乃分類為按攤銷成本計量的
          cost and their carrying amounts approximated their fair values.               金融資產，其賬面值與其公允價值相若。


          The Group applies the simplified approach to provide for expected             本集團應用國際財務報告準則第 9 號規定
          credit losses prescribed by IFRS 9, which permits the use of the lifetime     的簡易方法就預期信貸虧損計提撥備，該
          expected loss provision for all trade receivables. To measure the             準則允許為所有貿易應收款項使用整個存
          expected credit losses, trade receivables have been grouped based on          續期的預期虧損撥備。為計量預期信貸虧
          shared credit risk characteristics and the days past due. The expected        損，貿易應收款項已根據共有信貸風險特
          credit losses also incorporate forward looking information.                   徵及逾期天數分組。預期信貸虧損亦包含
                                                                                        前瞻性資料。

      30. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                    30. 關聯方交易
          Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly    倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
          or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence       能對另一方的財務及經營決策行使重大影
          over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties      響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方受共
          are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.       同控制，亦被視為關聯方。本集團主要管
          Members of key management and their close family member of the                 理層成員及彼等的近親亦被視為關聯方。
          Group are also considered as related parties.


          Save as disclosed elsewhere in this Interim Financial Information, the         除本中期財務資料其他部分所披露者外，
          following significant transactions were carried out between the Group          以下重大交易乃由本集團及其關聯方於截
          and its related parties during the six months ended June 30, 2025 and          至二零二五年及二零二四年六月三十日止
          2024. In the opinion of the Directors of the Company, the related party        六個月期間進行。本公司董事認為，該等
          transactions were carried out in the normal course of business and at          關聯方交易乃於日常業務過程中按本集團
          terms negotiated between the Group and the respective related parties.         與各自關聯方磋商的條款進行。
